We propose to combine the strengths of LL imitation learning for manipulation and control, and HL symbolic abstractions for long-horizon planning. We realise this idea via \emph{bilevel policies} of the form $(π^{\mathrm{hl}}, π^{\mathrm{ll}})$, consisting of a neural policy $π^{\mathrm{ll}}$ learned from LL demonstrations, and an HL symbolic policy $π^{\mathrm{hl}}$ that is constructed from symbolic abstractions of the LL demonstrations combined with inductive generalisation. We implement these ideas in the BISON system. Experiments on extended MetaWorld benchmarks demonstrate that BISON generalises to long horizons and problems with greater numbers of objects than those solved by VLA and end-to-end methods, and is more time and memory efficient in training and inference. Notably, when ignoring LL execution, BISON's HL policies can solve HL problems with 10,000 relevant objects in under a minute. Project page: https://dillonzchen.github.io/bison

</details>


### [148] [Petri Net Induced Heuristic Search for Resource Constrained Scheduling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15983)
*Ido Lublin,Dor Atzmon,Izack Cohen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文将资源约束项目调度问题（RCPSP）建模为带资源的时间变迁Petri网的可达图上的最优搜索，使用相对延迟令牌使得调度决策对应状态空间的变迁触发。采用A*算法结合关键路径与资源下界启发式函数，并证明该启发式在令牌时间语义下是一致的。在PSPLIB基准上的实验表明，该方法在成功率和求解时间上均优于强MIP基线（SCIP、CBC）。逐实例分析显示，启发式搜索与MIP沿着独立维度退化——A*受资源紧度影响，MIP受问题规模影响，资源强度调节两种求解器在规模上的优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RCPSP求解方法（如MIP）在问题规模增大时性能退化明显，亟需一种更高效的精确求解方法。

Method: 将RCPSP建模为带资源的时间变迁Petri网的可达图搜索问题，采用相对延迟令牌机制；使用A*算法，结合关键路径下界与资源下界的启发式函数，并证明其一致性。

Result: 在PSPLIB基准上，该方法在成功率和求解时间上均优于SCIP和CBC两种MIP求解器。A*性能受资源紧度影响，MIP受问题规模影响，资源强度调节二者的相对优势。

Conclusion: 基于Petri网可达图搜索的A*方法是一种有效的RCPSP精确求解方案，与MIP形成互补，在不同资源特性下各有优势。

Abstract: We formulate the Resource-Constrained Project Scheduling Problem (RCPSP) as optimal search over the reachability graph of a Timed Transition Petri Net with Resources, using relative-delay tokens so that scheduling decisions correspond to transition firings in the induced state space. We solve the resulting problem with $A^*$ guided by a heuristic that combines Critical Path and resource-based lower bounds, and prove that it is consistent under our token-based time semantics. Experiments on the PSPLIB benchmarks show that the approach outperforms strong exact Mixed-Integer Linear Programming (MIP) baselines (SCIP, CBC) in both success rate and solve time. Per-instance analysis shows that heuristic search and MIP degrade along independent axes, resource tightness for $A^*$ and formulation size for MIP, with resource strength mediating which solver benefits from scale.

</details>


### [149] [ScreenSearch: Uncertainty-Aware OS Exploration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16024)
*Michael Solodko,Justin Wagle*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出ScreenSearch系统，通过结构化的屏幕检索与去重、结合PUCT图-bandit的歧义感知搜索，解决桌面GUI智能体在部分可观测环境中的状态探索问题。系统在11个应用中收集超100万截图和3万去重状态，揭示了探索中的新颖性-歧义权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 桌面GUI智能体面临部分可观测性问题：视觉相似的屏幕可能对应不同的底层工作流状态，导致局部合理的动作可能产生截然不同的结果。需要将这一问题形式化为计算机/操作系统状态探索，有效行为需要同时扩展可达边界和减少歧义。

Method: 提出ScreenSearch系统，包含：（1）结构化的屏幕检索与去重层，将UIA树转换为位置感知的结构化特征，通过稀疏令牌搜索和元数据过滤器索引相关屏幕，并在VM工作节点间维护共享去重状态图；（2）基于匹配动作结果离散度的可扩展歧义信号，当相似屏幕在相同动作签名下产生不同下一状态时，需进一步探测；（3）结合歧义信号和前沿奖励驱动大规模探索和重放-启动策略评估。

Result: 在11个桌面应用中收集了超过100万张截图和超过3万个去重状态，产生了大量跨应用和跨应用内的多样化探索语料库。在固定的重放-启动切片上观察到清晰的新颖性-歧义权衡：某些策略快速减少歧义但发现的前沿状态很少。附录消融实验表明更强的提议先验可以实质性地改善语料库构建中的唯一状态发现。

Conclusion: 状态身份识别、提议质量和歧义感知搜索在决定何时探测和何时承诺时都很重要。歧义减少本身不是一个充分的探索目标。

Abstract: Desktop GUI agents operate under partial observability: visually similar screens can correspond to different underlying workflow states, so locally plausible actions can lead to sharply different outcomes. We frame this as a problem of computer/OS state exploration, where effective behavior requires both expanding the reachable frontier and reducing ambiguity before committing. We present ScreenSearch, a system that combines structural screen retrieval and deduplication with an ambiguity-aware PUCT graph-bandit for large-scale desktop exploration. The retrieval layer converts UIA trees into location-aware structural features, indexes related screens through sparse token search and metadata filters, and maintains a shared deduplicated state graph across VM workers. On top of this graph, we define a scalable ambiguity signal based on matched-action outcome dispersion. If similar screens produce different next states under the same action signature, the state should be probed further rather than treated as resolved. We use this signal together with frontier rewards to drive large-scale exploration and replay-start policy evaluation over the shared graph. Across 11 desktop applications, ScreenSearch collects over 1M screenshots and over 30K deduplicated states, yielding large exploration corpora with substantial cross-application and within-application diversity. On a fixed replay-start slice, we observe a clear novelty--ambiguity trade-off: some policies reduce ambiguity quickly while discovering little frontier. Ambiguity reduction alone is therefore not a sufficient exploration objective. Appendix ablations show that stronger proposal priors can materially improve unique-state discovery during corpus building. These results suggest that state identity, proposal quality, and ambiguity-aware search all matter when deciding when to probe and when to commit.

</details>


### [150] [Reasoners or Translators? Contamination-aware Evaluation and Neuro-Symbolic Robustness in Tax Law](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16052)
*Parisa Kordjamshidi,Samer Aslan,Madhavan Seshadri,Leslie Barrett,Enrico Santus*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文通过污染检测协议发现LLM在税法推理中的性能存在数据污染导致的虚高，并系统比较了单一LLM与神经符号混合系统，证明后者在组合性法律推理和未见过案例泛化方面更可靠。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究LLM在法律推理中的高性能是否源于真正的推理能力还是数据污染，并寻找更可靠的解决方案。

Method: 设计了污染检测协议评估LLM可靠性；构建了包含案例和规则变体的新型测试集；系统比较了单一LLM与将法条翻译为形式表示并交由符号求解器推理的神经符号混合系统。

Result: 数据污染会虚增LLM性能；法律推理本质上是组合性的；神经符号框架在可靠性、鲁棒性和未见过情况下的泛化能力显著优于单一LLM。

Conclusion: 神经符号系统为法律AI提供了更可靠的基础，能够更好地泛化到未见场景，而纯LLM方法受数据污染影响存在性能虚高问题。

Abstract: Recent advances in large language models (LLMs) have significantly enhanced automated legal reasoning. Yet, it remains unclear whether their performance reflects genuine legal reasoning ability or artifacts of data contamination. We present a comprehensive empirical study of tax law reasoning approaches and implement a contamination detection protocol to rigorously assess LLM reliability. We show that performance can be inflated by contamination. Building on this analysis, we conduct a systematic evaluation, comparing monolithic LLMs with hybrid systems that translate statutory text into formal representations and delegate inference to symbolic solvers. We build a novel test suite designed to probe generalization to unseen documents via case and rule variations. Our findings indicate that legal reasoning is inherently compositional and that neuro-symbolic frameworks offer a more reliable and robust foundation for legal AI, as well as improved generalization to unobserved situations.

</details>


### [151] [Sign-Separated Finite-Time Error Analysis of Q-Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16103)
*Donghwan Lee*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文对常数步长Q-learning进行了符号分离的有限时间误差分析，揭示了误差动态中由最大值算子引起的非对称性，并证明了负向误差由最优策略下的LTI系统主导、正向误差由切换系统控制，从而解释了Q-learning的过估计现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有Q-learning收敛分析通常忽略误差的正负符号差异，无法解释Q-learning固有的过估计偏差。本文旨在通过符号分离的方法，揭示误差动态中的非对称性，并从理论上解释最大值算子如何导致正向误差被选择和传播。

Method: 将Q-learning误差动态建模为切换系统，将误差分解为分量意义上的负部和正部；负部由固定最优策略下的低比较LTI系统主导，正部由线性切换系统控制；分别对确定性和随机常数步长递归进行有限时间界分析。

Result: 负向LTI证书的指数包络不慢于正向切换证书，且可能更快；最大值诱导的非对称性导致正向动作误差被选择并传播（即过估计），而负向误差可由最优策略提供下界比较。

Conclusion: Q-learning误差动态存在由Bellman最大值引起的非对称性，这直接关联到过估计问题；符号分离的分析框架为理解Q-learning的收敛行为和偏差提供了新视角。

Abstract: This paper develops a sign-separated finite-time error analysis for constant step-size Q-learning. Starting from the switching-system representation, the error is decomposed into its componentwise negative and positive parts. The negative part is dominated by a lower comparison linear time-invariant (LTI) system associated with a fixed optimal policy, whereas the positive part is controlled by a linear switching system. The resulting bounds show that the negative-side LTI certificate is no slower than the positive-side switching certificate and may produce a faster exponential envelope. The analysis identifies a max-induced asymmetry in Q-learning error dynamics. This asymmetry is connected to overestimation: positive action-wise errors can be selected and propagated by the Bellman maximum, whereas negative errors admit an optimal-policy lower comparison. Finite-time bounds are provided for both deterministic and stochastic constant-step-size recursions.

</details>


### [152] [Property-Guided LLM Program Synthesis for Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16142)
*Augusto B. Corrêa,André G. Pereira,Jendrik Seipp*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出属性引导的LLM程序合成方法，通过形式化属性检查和具体反例反馈，替代传统评分机制，在PDDL规划领域显著减少程序生成次数和评估成本，生成的启发式函数几乎在所有测试任务上有效，相比最优方法平均生成程序数量减少7倍，计算成本降低几个数量级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统LLM程序合成依赖简单数值评分（如解的质量或通过测试数），分数无法解释程序失败原因，导致系统需要生成和评估大量候选程序，增加了LLM推理和评估成本。本文旨在通过属性引导的方式解决这一效率问题。

Method: 提出属性引导的LLM程序合成方法：不依赖评分，而是检查候选程序是否满足形式化定义的属性；当属性被违反时，提前停止评估并向LLM提供具体反例，展示程序失败的确切方式；采用反例引导的修复循环，生成一个候选程序，在训练集上检查属性，返回第一个违反属性的案例。

Result: 在10个PDDL规划领域上评估，合成的启发式函数在几乎所有测试任务上有效；相比最佳先验方法，平均每个域生成的程序数量减少7倍，在更多任务上无需搜索即可求解，候选程序评估计算量降低几个数量级。

Conclusion: 只要问题存在可验证的属性，属性引导的LLM程序合成就能降低程序生成成本并提高程序质量。

Abstract: LLMs have shown impressive success in program synthesis, discovering programs that surpass prior solutions. However, these approaches rely on simple numeric scores to signal program quality, such as the value of the solution or the number of passed tests. Because a score offers no guidance on why a program failed, the system must generate and evaluate many candidates hoping some succeed, increasing LLM inference and evaluation costs. We study a different approach: property-guided LLM program synthesis. Instead of scoring programs after evaluation, we check whether a candidate satisfies a formally defined property. When the property is violated, we stop the evaluation early and provide the LLM with a concrete counterexample showing exactly how the program failed. This feedback drastically reduces both the number of program generations and the evaluation cost, and can guide the LLM to generate stronger programs. We evaluate this approach on PDDL planning domains, asking the LLM to synthesize direct heuristic functions: every state reachable by strictly improving transitions has a strictly improving successor. A heuristic with this property leads hill-climbing algorithm directly to a goal state. A counterexample-guided repair loop generates one candidate program, checks the property over a training set, and returns the first case that violates the property. We evaluate our approach on ten planning domains with an out-of-distribution test set. The synthesized heuristics are effectively direct on virtually all test tasks, and compared to the best prior generation method our approach generates seven times fewer programs per domain on average, solves more tasks without using search, and requires several orders of magnitude less computation to evaluate candidates. Whenever a problem admits a verifiable property, property-guided LLM synthesis can reduce cost and improve program quality.

</details>


### [153] [Look Before You Leap: Autonomous Exploration for LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16143)
*Ziang Ye,Wentao Shi,Yuxin Liu,Yu Wang,Zhengzhou Cai,Yaorui Shi,Qi Gu,Xunliang Cai,Fuli Feng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出探索检查点覆盖率（ECC）指标量化智能体的探索能力，并设计任务-探索交错训练策略与"先探索后行动"范式，证明系统性探索对构建泛化智能体的必要性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于LLM的智能体在陌生环境中常因过早利用先验知识而失败，而自主探索作为构建自适应智能体的关键能力尚未被充分研究。

Method: 提出探索检查点覆盖率（ECC）作为可验证的探索广度指标；设计任务执行轨迹与探索轨迹交错训练的策略，每种轨迹由对应的可验证奖励优化；构建"先探索后行动"范式，将信息收集与任务执行解耦。

Result: 标准任务导向强化学习训练的智能体表现出狭窄重复的行为模式并损害下游性能。交错训练策略有效提升探索覆盖率，进而改善任务执行表现。

Conclusion: 学习系统性探索对于构建可泛化、面向真实世界的智能体至关重要。

Abstract: Large language model based agents often fail in unfamiliar environments due to premature exploitation: a tendency to act on prior knowledge before acquiring sufficient environment-specific information. We identify autonomous exploration as a critical yet underexplored capability for building adaptive agents. To formalize and quantify this capability, we introduce Exploration Checkpoint Coverage, a verifiable metric that measures how broadly an agent discovers key states, objects, and affordances. Our systematic evaluation reveals that agents trained with standard task-oriented reinforcement learning consistently exhibit narrow and repetitive behaviors that impede downstream performance. To address this limitation, we develop a training strategy that interleaves task-execution rollouts and exploration rollouts, with each type of rollout optimized by its corresponding verifiable reward. Building on this training strategy, we propose the Explore-then-Act paradigm, which decouples information-gathering from task execution: agents first utilize an interaction budget to acquire grounded environmental knowledge, then leverage it for task resolution. Our results demonstrate that learning to systematically explore is imperative for building generalizable and real-world-ready agents.

</details>


### [154] [An Algebraic Exposition of the Theory of Dyadic Morality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16153)
*Kush R. Varshney*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文使用结构因果建模（SCM）形式化二元道德理论（TDM），通过类型化、补全和效价依赖推理三种心理算子扩展标准SCM，并利用节点坍缩和顺序处理解决可扩展性问题，最后展示了该框架在AI政策设计中的应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有道德判断的心理模型缺乏正式化的数学框架，TDM虽然简洁但存在二元性局限（仅考虑故意施害者与受害者的双节点模板），难以扩展到多节点真实场景；同时需要一种既能忠实反映人类道德认知又具备计算可操作性的形式化方法。

Method: 1) 使用SCM符号系统形式化TDM的双节点模板；2) 定义三种心理算子：类型化算子（角色指派）、补全算子（缺失角色推理）和效价依赖推理机制（基于结果效价的因果回溯）；3) 通过节点坍缩（多节点压缩为双节点）和顺序处理（时序分解）解决可扩展性问题；4) 推荐情境化、范围受限的心智感知测量方法。

Result: 形式化框架成功应用于三个AI政策场景：检测冲突义务、构建保持用户自主性的帮助策略、以及将失败后沟通设计为因果干预。证明了代数框架能同时保证数学严谨性和对人类道德认知的忠实性。

Conclusion: 该代数形式化为神经符号AI系统提供了计算道德判断的数学基础，使AI能够以可解释、可扩展的方式处理道德推理，同时建议在实证操作化中采用情境化的心智感知测量而非全局平均。

Abstract: This paper provides an algebraic exposition of the theory of dyadic morality (TDM), a psychological model of moral judgment grounded in a simple two-node template: an intentional agent causing harm to a vulnerable patient. We formalize TDM using structural causal modeling (SCM) notation and identify three psychological operators (typecasting operator, completion operator, and valence-dependent inference mechanism) that extend standard SCM to capture how people compute moral judgments under constraints. We address scalability challenges arising from TDM's dyadic limitation, showing how moral cognition compresses multi-node scenarios through node collapse and sequential processing. Drawing on this algebraic framework, we demonstrate concrete applications to AI policy design: detecting conflicting obligations, structuring helpfulness policies to preserve user agency, and designing post-failure communication as causal interventions. Finally, we recommend scoped, contextual measurement of mind perception over universal averaging to operationalize the theory empirically. This algebraic formalization enables neurosymbolic AI systems to compute morality in a way that is both mathematically rigorous and faithful to human moral cognition.

</details>


### [155] [Formal Methods Meet LLMs: Auditing, Monitoring, and Intervention for Compliance of Advanced AI Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16198)
*Parand A. Alamdari,Toryn Q. Klassen,Sheila A. McIlraith*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出结合形式化方法（LTL）与机器学习的技术，用于在AI开发生命周期（从部署前测试到部署后审计）中对黑盒AI系统（尤其是LLM）进行离线审计与在线运行时监测，并实验证明该方法在检测时序行为约束违规方面优于LLM基线方法，且预测性和干预性监测器能显著降低违规率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI治理中对AI产品和服务进行全生命周期监测与审计的需求日益迫切，但现有方法难以有效检测AI系统（尤其是LLM）在长时序、多约束条件下的行为违规。

Method: 结合形式化方法中的线性时序逻辑（LTL）语法和语义与SoTA机器学习，提出针对黑盒AI系统的离线审计和在线运行时监测技术，包括基于采样的预测性监测和运行时干预监测器。

Result: 1) 所提方法在检测时序扩展行为约束违规方面优于LLM基线方法，小模型标注器可达到或超过前沿LLM裁判；2) 预测性和干预性监测器显著降低LLM智能体的违规率，同时保持任务性能；3) LLM时序推理能力随事件距离、约束数量和命题数量增加而显著下降。

Conclusion: 基于形式化LTL的审计与监测方法能有效弥补纯LLM方法在时序行为约束检测上的不足，为AI治理提供可靠的技术支撑。

Abstract: We examine one particular dimension of AI governance: how to monitor and audit AI-enabled products and services throughout the AI development lifecycle, from pre-deployment testing to post-deployment auditing. Combining principles from formal methods with SoTA machine learning, we propose techniques that enable AI-enabled product and service developers, as well as third party AI developers and evaluators, to perform offline auditing and online (runtime) monitoring of product-specific (temporally extended) behavioral constraints such as safety constraints, norms, rules and regulations with respect to black-box advanced AI systems, notably LLMs. We further provide practical techniques for predictive monitoring, such as sampling-based methods, and we introduce intervening monitors that act at runtime to preempt and potentially mitigate predicted violations. Experimental results show that by exploiting the formal syntax and semantics of Linear Temporal Logic (LTL), our proposed auditing and monitoring techniques are superior to LLM baseline methods in detecting violations of temporally extended behavioral constraints; with our approach, even small-model labelers match or exceed frontier LLM judges. Our predictive and intervening monitors significantly reduce the violation rates of LLM-based agents while largely preserving task performance. We further show through controlled experiments that LLMs' temporal reasoning shows a pronounced degradation in accuracy with increasing event distance, number of constraints, and number of propositions.

</details>


### [156] [Fully Open Meditron: An Auditable Pipeline for Clinical LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16215)
*Xavier Theimer-Lienhard,Mushtaha El-Amin,Fay Elhassan,Sahaj Vaidya,Victor Cartier-Negadi,David Sasu,Lars Klein,Mary-Anne Hartley*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了Fully Open Meditron，第一个用于构建LLM临床决策支持系统(CDSS)的完全开源管道，包括经临床医生审核的训练语料库、可复现的数据构建和训练框架，以及对齐使用的评估协议。基于五个完全开源基础模型的应用表明，完全开源管道可以在不牺牲可审计性和可复现性的前提下达到领域特定的最优性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于LLM的CDSS大多不透明，即使是"开放"模型也只是开放权重，不公开数据来源、筛选流程和生成管道。医学领域缺乏完全开放（Fully Open）的模型，导致无法实现严谨、可复现的验证。

Method: 构建了由临床医生审核的训练语料库（统一8个公开医学QA数据集，并扩展3种临床医生验证的合成数据扩展）；设计了可复现的数据构建和训练框架（系统级去污染、金标签重采样教师生成、四名医生小组端到端验证）；采用LLM-as-a-judge评估协议，并经204名人类评分员校准。

Result: 将方法应用于五个完全开源基础模型（Apertus-70B/8B-Instruct等），所有MeditronFO变体均优于其基础模型。Apertus-70B-MeditronFO在医学基准上提升+6.6个百分点（47.2%→53.8%），达到新的FO最优。Gemma-3-27B-MeditronFO在58.6%的LLM-as-a-judge比较中优于MedGemma，并在HealthBench上胜出（58% vs 55.9%）。

Conclusion: 完全开源管道可以在不牺牲可审计性和可复现性的前提下，实现领域特定的最优性能。

Abstract: Clinical decision support systems (CDSS) require scrutable, auditable pipelines that enable rigorous, reproducible validation. Yet current LLM-based CDSS remain largely opaque. Most "open" models are open-weight only, releasing parameters while withholding the data provenance, curation procedures, and generation pipelines that determine model behavior. Fully Open (FO) models, which expose the complete training stack end-to-end, do not currently exist in medicine. We introduce Fully Open Meditron, the first fully open pipeline for building LLM-CDSS, comprising a clinician-audited training corpus, a reproducible data construction and training framework, and a use-aligned evaluation protocol. The corpus unifies eight public medical QA datasets into a normalized conversational format and expands coverage with three clinician-vetted synthetic extensions: exam-style QA, guideline-grounded QA derived from 46,469 clinical practice guidelines, and clinical vignettes. The pipeline enforces system-wide decontamination, gold-label resampling of teacher generations, and end-to-end validation by a four-physician panel. We evaluate using an LLM-as-a-judge protocol over expert-written clinical vignettes, calibrated against 204 human raters. We apply the recipe to five FO base models (Apertus-70B/8B-Instruct, OLMo-2-32B-SFT, EuroLLM-22B/9B-Instruct). All MeditronFO variants are preferred over their bases. Apertus-70B-MeditronFO improves +6.6 points over its base (47.2% to 53.8%) on aggregate medical benchmarks, establishing a new FO SoTA. Gemma-3-27B-MeditronFO is preferred over MedGemma in 58.6% of LLM-as-a-judge comparisons and outperforms it on HealthBench (58% vs 55.9%). These results show that fully open pipelines can achieve state-of-the-art domain-specific performance without sacrificing auditability or reproducibility.

</details>


### [157] [Prospective multi-pathogen disease forecasting using autonomous LLM-guided tree search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16238)
*Sarah Martinson,Michael P. Brenner,Martyna Plomecka,Brian P. Williams,Nicholas G. Reich,Zahra Shamsi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于大语言模型引导的树搜索自主系统，能够自动生成、评估和优化传染病概率预测软件。在2025-2026年美国呼吸道季节的前瞻性实时评估中，该系统自主发现的模型集成在流感、COVID-19和RSV预测上匹配或超越了CDC人工策划的金标准集成模型。该方法通过优化对数尺度距离指标防止奖励黑客攻击，并利用自动评审机制确保对复杂科学理论的结构保真度，克服了建模劳动力瓶颈，实现了专家级疾病预测的快速大规模部署。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传染病概率预测对公共卫生至关重要，但依赖专家团队的劳动密集型人工模型策划，这种定制开发模式限制了预测向细粒度地理分辨率或新兴病原体的可扩展性。

Method: 提出LLM引导的树搜索自主系统，通过迭代生成、评估和优化可执行预测软件。采用对数尺度距离指标优化防止奖励黑客攻击，并引入自动评审机制确保对科学理论的结构保真度。

Result: 在2025-2026年美国呼吸道季节的前瞻性实时评估中，系统自主发现的模型集成在流感、COVID-19和RSV预测上持续匹配或超越CDC人工策划的金标准集成模型；成功应对RSV的"冷启动"数据稀缺场景。

Conclusion: 通过将流行病学理论自主转化为准确、透明的代码，该框架克服了建模劳动力瓶颈，能够以前所未有的规模快速部署专家级疾病预测。

Abstract: Probabilistic forecasting of infectious diseases is crucial for public health but relies on labor-intensive manual model curation by expert modeling teams. This bespoke development bottlenecks scalability to granular geographic resolutions or emerging pathogens. Here, we present an autonomous system using Large Language Model (LLM)-guided tree search to iteratively generate, evaluate, and optimize executable forecasting software. In a fully prospective, real-time evaluation during the 2025-2026 US respiratory season, the system autonomously discovered methodologically diverse models for influenza, COVID-19, and respiratory syncytial virus (RSV). Aggregating these machine-generated models yielded an ensemble that consistently matched or outperformed the gold-standard, human-curated Centers for Disease Control and Prevention (CDC) hub ensembles out-of-sample. The system successfully navigated data-scarce "cold start" scenarios for RSV. Moreover, controlled retrospective ablations revealed that optimizing log-scale distance metrics prevents reward hacking, while an automated judge-in-the-loop ensures structural fidelity to complex scientific theories. By autonomously translating epidemiological theory into accurate, transparent code, this framework overcomes the modeling labor bottleneck, enabling rapid deployment of expert-level disease forecasting at unprecedented scales.

</details>


<div id='cs.GR'></div>

# cs.GR [[Back]](#toc)

### [158] [FFAvatar: Few-Shot, Feed-Forward, and Generalizable Avatar Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15320)
*Thuan Hoang Nguyen,Jiahao Luo,Yinyu Nie,Hao Li,Gordon Guocheng Qian,Jian Wang*

Main category: cs.GR

TL;DR: FFAvatar是一个前馈式3D高斯头部分身重建框架，能从少量未摆姿势的肖像图像中快速重建可动画的高质量3D高斯头部分身，无需针对个体的优化或昂贵的预处理，在身份保持、几何一致性和动画保真度方面达到新标准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统分身重建依赖耗时（数小时）的逐主体优化或昂贵的预处理，限制了可扩展性。本文旨在提出一种通用的前馈框架，实现从少量未摆姿势图像中快速（秒级）重建高质量、可动画的3D头部分身。

Method: 提出FFAvatar框架，核心包括：(1) Multi-View Query-Former：将多源图像信息融合为统一的规范高斯表示；(2) 端到端从像素预测FLAME参数，避免离线FLAME提取的开销；(3) 三阶段训练策略：大规模单目视频预训练（超100万身份）、小样本高质量360度多视图微调、可选个性化优化（500步内）。

Result: 在NeRSemble基准上以5.5 PSNR的大幅增益超越SOTA方法LAM；可在2秒（无个性化）或10秒（有个性化）内重建分身；支持在单个NVIDIA A100 GPU上以49 FPS进行实时动画。

Conclusion: FFAvatar通过前馈框架结合三阶段训练，实现了强泛化能力与高保真重建的平衡，为实时、可扩展的头部分身重建设立了新标准。

Abstract: Avatar reconstruction has traditionally relied on per-subject optimization that requires hours of computation or on expensive preprocessing that limits scalability. We introduce FFAvatar, a generalizable feed-forward framework that reconstructs high-quality, animatable 3D Gaussian head avatars from few-shot unposed portrait images in seconds. FFAvatar fuses information from multiple source images into a unified canonical Gaussian representation through Multi-View Query-Former, which is animated via FLAME parameters predicted end-to-end directly from pixels, eliminating the overhead of offline FLAME extraction. We further propose a three-stage training curriculum that achieves both broad generalization and high-fidelity reconstruction: (i) scalable pretraining on extensive monocular video data with over 1M identities to learn strong generalizable priors; (ii) multi-view fine-tuning on a small but high-quality dataset of 360-degree captures to enhance geometric fidelity and extreme-view awareness; and (iii) optional personalization that adapts to specific identities for maximum fidelity within 500 optimization steps. Extensive experiments demonstrate that FFAvatar sets a new standard for identity preservation, geometric consistency, and animation fidelity. On the NeRSemble benchmark, it outperforms the state-of-the-art LAM by a substantial 5.5 PSNR gain. Furthermore, FFAvatar enables real-time deployment, reconstructing avatars in 2 seconds without personalization and 10 seconds with personalization, while supporting 49 FPS animation on a single NVIDIA A100 GPU.

</details>


### [159] [StippleDiffusion: Capacity-Constrained Stippling using Controlled Diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15816)
*Ofir Gilad,Aleksander Plocharski,Przemyslaw Musialski,Andrei Sharf*

Main category: cs.GR

TL;DR: 本文提出首个基于扩散模型的图像条件化点画生成方法，通过ControlNet分支结合最优传输网格扩散基线，在推理时同时满足学习到的局部点分布先验和图像定义的容量约束，在Icons-50基准上达到与逐密度优化基线相当的性能，且保持端到端可微。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统点画模式生成依赖逐密度迭代优化器，速度慢、不可微且每次需从头运行；现有学习方法仅能处理无条件点生成，无法解决容量受限的图像条件化点画问题。

Method: 构建基于最优传输网格点集扩散基线的ControlNet分支，以目标密度图和高分辨率图像为条件；限制训练和推理在晚期去噪阶段，从密度加权拒绝采样初始化；用sigmoid门控1x1投影替代标准零卷积注入，以在硬密度信号下保持基模型的蓝噪声结构。

Result: 单个训练好的检查点可接受任意目标密度进行推理，泛化到未见过的点预算，生成时间几乎与输出点数无关；在Icons-50基准上，所有报告指标均达到与逐密度优化基线相当的水平。

Conclusion: 所提方法为可微、高效、图像条件化的点画生成提供了可行的扩散式解决方案，兼具传统优化的质量和学习的灵活性。

Abstract: Stipple patterns, point sets whose local density tracks a target image, are traditionally produced by per-density iterative optimizers, which are slow, non-differentiable, and must be re-run from scratch for each new target. Learned alternatives have so far addressed only unconditional point generation; capacity-constrained, image-conditioned stippling has remained out of reach. We present the first diffusion-based sampler that simultaneously satisfies a learned local point-distribution prior and a continuous, image-defined capacity constraint at inference. The method is a ControlNet branch built on top of an optimal-transport-grid point-set diffusion baseline, conditioned on the target density map and a high-resolution image. Two design choices make the combination tractable: training and inference are restricted to the late-stage denoising regime, initialized from a density-weighted rejection sample, and the standard zero-convolution injection is replaced with a sigmoid-gated 1x1 projection that preserves the base model's blue-noise structure under hard density signals. A single trained checkpoint accepts arbitrary target densities at inference, generalizes to point budgets that were not seen during training, and produces stipples in time nearly independent of the output point count. On the Icons-50 benchmark, our learned sampler reaches parity with per-density-optimized baselines on every reported metric while remaining differentiable end-to-end.

</details>


### [160] [Distributed Affine Body Dynamics with Adaptive Consensus](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15875)
*Jiafeng Liu,Wenhui Zhou,Xinming Pei,Yifan Peng,Huamin Wang,Yin Yang,Lei Lan,Weiwei Xu*

Main category: cs.GR

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于共识ADMM的分布式仿射体动力学（ABD）方法，在增量势接触（IPC）框架下实现多GPU/多节点的高效并行模拟，同时保持非穿透约束和全局一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: IPC框架中全局耦合的势垒约束阻碍了其在多GPU和多计算节点上的可扩展执行，现有方法难以兼顾刚性固体的精确模拟和大规模分布式计算需求。

Method: 采用基于共识的交替方向乘子法（ADMM）对ABD进行分布式求解：每个计算节点并行求解局部ABD子问题，然后通过全局共识步骤强制共享边界体之间的一致性。

Result: 实验证明该方法具有稳定的收敛性、非穿透保证，并在多节点大规模场景下实现了高效的扩展性。

Conclusion: 该分布式ABD方案在保持IPC级鲁棒性和全局一致性的前提下，成功突破了IPC在多GPU/多节点环境中的可扩展性瓶颈。

Abstract: Affine Body Dynamics (ABD) within the Incremental Potential Contact (IPC) framework provides accurate simulation of extremely stiff solids exhibiting near-rigid behavior, with strict non-penetration guarantees. However, IPC's globally coupled barrier constraints hinder scalable execution across multiple GPUs and compute nodes. We propose a distributed formulation of ABD using a consensus-based ADMM scheme. Each compute node solves its local ABD subproblem in parallel, followed by a global consensus step that enforces consistency among shared boundary bodies. The proposed method preserves IPC-level robustness and global consistency under distributed execution. Experiments demonstrate stable convergence, non-penetration, and efficient scaling on large-scale scenes across multiple nodes.

</details>


### [161] [Smart target point control for Gaussian Splatting methods](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16158)
*Pratik Singh Bisht,Andreas Kolb*

Main category: cs.GR

TL;DR: 提出了一种目标点控制方案，通过调整现有密集化和透明度剔除的超参数，跟踪二次目标计数轨迹，在15k迭代时达到所需高斯数量，确保不同方法获得相同的密集化和剪枝周期，实现更公平、容量匹配的评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准高斯泼溅方法依赖启发式密集化和剪枝来自适应分配图元，导致高斯数量同时影响重建质量和运行时间，使得跨方法比较变得不可靠——改进可能来自更高的表示容量而非算法设计。现有的硬停止或预算限制方法会导致训练偏差。

Method: 提出一种目标点控制方案（quota-governor），保留标准密集化窗口和节奏，仅调整现有的密集化和透明度剔除超参数，以跟踪二次目标计数轨迹，在15k迭代时平滑达到目标数量，无需突然截断。

Result: 确保所有方法和视图获得相等的密集化和剪枝周期，实现更公平、容量匹配的评估。

Conclusion: 该方案消除了因高斯数量差异导致的比较偏差，使不同方法的评估更公平可靠。

Abstract: Standard Gaussian splatting methods rely on heuristic densification and pruning to adaptively allocate primitives during training, and the resulting Gaussian count strongly influences both reconstruction quality and runtime. This makes comparisons across methods fragile: improvements can stem from higher representational capacity rather than algorithmic design. A common and naive workaround for this is hard-stopping or budgeting densification/pruning once a target count is reached, which biases training because different methods hit the cap at different times, yielding non-uniform densify/prune exposure across views and uneven point distributions. We propose a target point control scheme that preserves the standard densification window and cadence, but adjusts only the existing densification and opacity-culling hyper-parameters to track a quadratic target count trajectory. This quota-governor reaches the desired count by 15k iterations without abrupt cutoffs, ensuring that all methods and views receive equal densification and pruning cycles, enabling fairer, capacity-matched evaluation.

</details>


### [162] [Evaluating Design Video Generation: Metrics for Compositional Fidelity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.16223)
*Adrienne Deganutti,Dingning Cao,Jaejung Seol,Elad Hirsch,Purvanshi Mehta*

Main category: cs.GR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个面向设计动画领域的全自动评估框架，从布局保真度、运动正确性、时间质量和内容保真度四个维度进行标准化评估，替代了主观人工评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式视频模型在设计动画任务中的应用日益增多，但该领域缺乏标准化的评估框架。设计动画不同于自然视频生成，具有结构化约束（特定组件的运动类型、方向、速度、时序需精确控制，非动画区域需保持稳定，布局结构需保持完整），亟需一个统一的客观评估标准。

Method: 构建了一个全自动评估框架，涵盖四个评估维度：布局保真度（布局结构是否完整）、运动正确性（运动类型、方向、速度、时序是否准确）、时间质量（时序连贯性）和内容保真度（内容是否真实）。

Result: 提出了一个无需人工干预的自动化评估方案，为设计动画领域的基准测试提供了统一的评估基础。

Conclusion: 该评估框架消除了对主观人工评估的依赖，为设计动画生成模型的进展建立了可比较的共同基准。

Abstract: Generative video models are increasingly used in design animation tasks, yet no standardized evaluation framework exists for this domain. Unlike natural video generation, design animation imposes structured constraints: specific components shall animate with prescribed motion types, directions, speed and timing, while non-animated regions must remain stable and layout structure must be preserved. This paper provides a fully automated evaluation framework organized across four dimensions: layout fidelity, motion correctness, temporal quality, and content fidelity. This eliminates the reliance on subjective human evaluation and establishes a common basis for benchmarking progress in the field.

</details>


### [163] [Sound Sparks Motion: Audio and Text Tuning for Video Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15307)
*AmirHossein Naghi Razlighi,Aryan Mikaeili,Ali Mahdavi-Amiri,Daniel Cohen-Or,Yiorgos Chrysanthou*

Main category: cs.GR

TL;DR: Sound Sparks Motion 提出一种无需训练的测试时调优框架，通过仅调整音频潜在变量和文本条件的残差扰动，使现有音视频生成模型实现运动编辑，并利用视觉语言模型提供时序对齐反馈指导优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大尺度生成视频模型对运动编辑（如局部动作或状态转换）效果欠佳，仅靠文本提示难以精确控制视频中运动内容的变化。

Method: 训练自由的测试时调优方法：仅调整源视频的音频潜在变量和文本条件的残差扰动这两个轻量变量；使用视觉语言模型提供文本-运动时序对齐的反馈信号作为优化目标；结合正则化和感知-时序约束保持内容与视觉质量。

Result: 该方法能有效实现模型在纯文本控制下难以达成的运动编辑；习得的潜在控制可在视频间迁移，表明其捕捉了可复用的运动编辑方向而非过拟合单个样本；尤其通过音频路径的多模态条件调优展现了运动编辑潜力。

Conclusion: 多模态条件调优（尤其是音频路径）是运动感知视频编辑的有前景方向；测试时调优可作为轻量探针机制，揭示模型多模态条件中嵌入的潜在运动控制。

Abstract: Motion-centric video editing remains difficult for large generative video models, which often respond well to appearance changes but struggle to produce specific, localized actions or state transitions in an existing clip. We introduce Sound Sparks Motion, a training-free framework that enables motion editing in an audio-visual video generation model by tuning its internal multimodal conditioning signals at test time. Rather than modifying model weights, our method tunes only two lightweight variables: an audio latent derived from the source video and a residual perturbation in the text-conditioning. We find that this combination can encourage motion edits that the underlying model often struggles to realize under prompt-only control. Since there is no direct way to evaluate temporal alignment between text and motion, we guide the tuning process using a vision-language model that provides feedback indicating whether the intended motion appears in the generated video. This simple supervision yields an effective semantic objective for motion editing, while regularization and perceptual-temporal constraints help preserve content and visual quality. Beyond per-video tuning, we show that the learned latent controls are transferable across videos, suggesting that they capture reusable motion-edit directions rather than overfitting to a single example.
  Our results highlight multimodal conditioning tuning, particularly through the audio pathway, as a promising direction for motion-aware video editing, and suggest that test-time tuning can serve as a lightweight probing mechanism that helps reveal latent motion controls embedded in the model's multimodal conditioning. Code and data are available via our project page: https://amirhossein-razlighi.github.io/Sound_Sparks_Motion/

</details>


### [164] [3DEditSafe: Defending 3D Editing Pipelines from Unsafe Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15398)
*Nicole Meng,Zheyuan Liu,Meng Jiang,Yingjie Lao*

Main category: cs.GR

TL;DR: 本文首次系统研究文本驱动3D编辑流水线中的不安全生成问题，提出3DEditSafe框架，通过安全正则化约束优化过程中的不安全语义传播，在EditSplat场景上证明2D安全引导不足以防御3D编辑中的不安全内容，并揭示了安全性与质量之间的权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于3D高斯泼溅（3DGS）的3D生成编辑流水线在从文本提示实现高保真、多视图一致的场景编辑方面取得了显著进展，但这些流水线也引入了新的安全风险——当不安全提示产生的编辑在多视图间传播和优化时，可能导致最终的3D表示中出现连贯的、不良的NSFW内容。目前缺乏针对此类不安全生成问题的防御方法。

Method: 提出3DEditSafe安全正则化编辑框架，包含四个关键组件：生成阶段安全引导、渲染视图3D安全正则化、安全语义投影、残差抑制以及掩码感知保留，共同约束优化过程远离不安全编辑方向。

Result: 在EditSplat场景上使用对象兼容的不安全提示基准进行评估，证明仅依靠2D安全引导不足以一致地防止不安全3D编辑；3DEditSafe有效降低了不安全语义对齐度和视图级攻击成功率，同时揭示了更强的安全抑制可能引入伪影或降低不安全提示保真度的安全-质量权衡。

Conclusion: 本文是首个系统研究并防御文本驱动3D编辑流水线中不安全生成问题的工作，强调了需要在优化的3D表示上直接操作的安全机制。

Abstract: Recent advances in 3D generative editing, particularly pipelines based on 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS), have achieved high-fidelity, multi-view-consistent scene manipulation from text prompts. However, we find that these pipelines also introduce new safety risks when unsafe prompts produce edits that are propagated and optimized across views. In this work, we study unsafe generation in 3D editing pipelines and show that such behavior can lead to coherent, undesirable Not-Safe-For-Work (NSFW) content in the final 3D representation.
  To address this, we propose 3DEditSafe, a safety-regularized 3D editing framework that constrains unsafe semantic propagation during optimization. 3DEditSafe combines generation-stage safety guidance with rendered-view 3D safety regularization, safe semantic projection, residue suppression, and mask-aware preservation to steer optimization away from unsafe editing directions. We evaluate our approach on EditSplat scenes using an object-compatible unsafe prompt benchmark and show that 2D safety guidance alone is not consistently sufficient to prevent unsafe 3D edits. 3DEditSafe reduces unsafe semantic alignment and view-level attack success rates, while revealing a safety-quality tradeoff in which stronger unsafe suppression can introduce artifacts or reduce unsafe-prompt fidelity. To our knowledge, this work is the first attempt to study and defend against unsafe generation in text-driven 3D editing pipelines, highlighting the need for safety mechanisms that operate directly on optimized 3D representations.

</details>
